# ====================================
# SAKUYA BETA - Location Marker System
# ==================================== 
sakuya_beta_mark_location = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		owner = {
			is_human = yes			#原始字段 is_player = YES
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_marked
			value = yes
		}
	}
}

sakuya_beta_unmark_location = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_marked
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = sakuya_marked
	}
}

sakuya_beta_mark_province_all = {
	scope = province
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_location_in_province = {
			owner = {
				is_human = yes				#原始字段 is_player = YES
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					is_human = yes					#原始字段 is_player = YES
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_marked
				value = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_beta_unmark_province_all = {
	scope = province
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_location_in_province = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_province = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			remove_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
}

sakuya_beta_mark_region_all = {
	scope = region
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_location_in_region = {
			owner = {
				is_human = yes				#原始字段 is_player = YES
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_region = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					is_human = yes					#原始字段 is_player = YES
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_marked
				value = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# 标记玩家所有地点
sakuya_beta_mark_all_locations = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_marked
				value = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# 取消标记玩家所有地点
sakuya_beta_unmark_all_locations = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			remove_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# SAKUYA AFTERBURNER - Foreign Location Marker System
# ====================================
sakuya_afterburner_mark_foreign_location = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		owner = {
			is_human = no
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			value = yes
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_unmark_foreign_location = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		owner = {
			is_human = no
		}
		has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_mark_foreign_province_all = {
	scope = province
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_location_in_province = {
			owner = {
				is_human = no
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					is_human = no
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
				value = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_mark_foreign_owner_all_locations = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		owner = {
			is_human = no
			any_owned_location = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		owner = {
			every_owned_location = {
				set_variable = {
					name = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
					value = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_unmark_foreign_owner_all_locations = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		owner = {
			is_human = no
			any_owned_location = {
				has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		owner = {
			every_owned_location = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
				}
				remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_annex_foreign_location = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		exists = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		owner = {
			is_human = no
		}
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
		change_location_owner = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_annex_foreign_province_all = {
	scope = province
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		exists = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		any_location_in_province = {
			owner = {
				is_human = no
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					is_human = no
				}
			}
			remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			change_location_owner = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_annex_foreign_area_all = {
	scope = area
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		exists = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		any_location_in_area = {
			owner = {
				is_human = no
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_area = {
			limit = {

					is_human = no
				}
			}
			remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			change_location_owner = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_annex_foreign_region_all = {
	scope = region
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		exists = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		any_location_in_region = {
			owner = {
				is_human = no
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_location_in_region = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					is_human = no
				}
			}
			remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			change_location_owner = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_annex_foreign_owner_all_locations = {
	scope = location
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		exists = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
		owner = {
			is_human = no
			any_owned_location = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		owner = {
			save_scope_as = sakuya_afterburner_annex_target_country
		}
		scope:sakuya_afterburner_annex_target_country = {
			every_owned_location = {
				remove_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
				change_location_owner = scope:sakuya_afterburner_player_country
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_afterburner_check_has_foreign_marked = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_owned_location = {
				has_variable = sakuya_afterburner_foreign_marked
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 国家标记系统 - Country Marker System
# ====================================

# 标记国家（自动取消其他国家标记）
sakuya_beta_mark_country = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_marked_country }
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
		}
	}
	effect = {
		# 清除所有国家的两个变量
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked_country }
			remove_variable = sakuya_marked_country
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
			remove_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
		# 同时标记当前国家（两个变量）
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_marked_country
			value = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			value = yes
		}
	}
}

# 取消标记国家
sakuya_beta_unmark_country = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		OR = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked_country
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = sakuya_marked_country
		remove_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
	}
}

# ====================================
# 简单人口添加系统 - 24个直接SGUI
# 固定50%识字率，8职业×3人数（100/1000/10000）
# ====================================
# 劳工 (Laborers)
sakuya_add_pop_laborers_100 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_pop = {
				culture = scope:player_country.culture
				religion = scope:player_country.religion
				type = pop_type:laborers
				size = 0.1
				literacy = 0.5
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_add_pop_laborers_1000 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_pop = {
				culture = scope:player_country.culture
				religion = scope:player_country.religion
				type = pop_type:laborers
				size = 1
				literacy = 0.5
			}
		}
	}
}

# 神职人员 (Clerics)
sakuya_add_pop_clerics_100 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_gold = 100
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_pop = {
				culture = scope:player_country.culture
				religion = scope:player_country.religion
				type = pop_type:clergy
				size = 0.1
				literacy = 0.5
			}
		}
	}
}

# 农民 (Peasants)

# ====================================
# Location Test Buttons
# ====================================
# 按钮1：给所有标记地点添加人口增长modifier
sakuya_loc_btn1_add_mod = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_location_modifier = {
				mode = add_and_extend
				modifier = sakuya_location_pop_growth
				years = -1
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮1右键：移除人口增长modifier
sakuya_loc_btn1_remove_mod = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			remove_location_modifier = sakuya_location_pop_growth
		}
	}
}

# 按钮2：给标记地点添加888人口（玩家文化+宗教，劳工）
sakuya_loc_btn2_add_pop = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_pop = {
				culture = scope:player_country.culture
				religion = scope:player_country.religion
				type = pop_type:laborers
				size = 0.888
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮3：将标记地点控制力设置为100
sakuya_loc_btn3_set_control = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_control = control_radical_bonus
		}
	}
}

# 按钮4：核心化标记地点
sakuya_loc_btn4_add_core = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_core = root
		}
	}
}

# 按钮4右键：取消核心化
sakuya_loc_btn4_remove_core = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			remove_core = root
		}
	}
}

# 按钮5：将标记地点所有人口的宗教转换为玩家国家的宗教
sakuya_loc_btn5_convert_religion = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			every_pop = {
				split_pop = {
					fraction = 1
					religion = scope:player_country.religion
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮6：将标记地点所有人口的文化同化为玩家的主流文化
sakuya_loc_btn6_convert_culture = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			every_pop = {
				split_pop = {
					fraction = 1
					culture = scope:player_country.culture
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮7：增加标记地点的发展度
sakuya_loc_btn7_add_development = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_development = development_severe_bonus
		}
	}
}

# 按钮8左键：将标记地点改为城市
sakuya_loc_btn8_upgrade_city = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_location_rank = location_rank:city
		}
	}
}

# 按钮8右键：将标记地点改为集镇
sakuya_loc_btn8_downgrade_town = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_location_rank = location_rank:town
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# HOME页面新按钮组 (保留旧按钮1-8的SGUI和modifier以保持兼容性)
# ====================================
# HOME按钮1：添加唯贤主义思潮
sakuya_loc_home_btn1_convert_culture = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_institution_progress = {
				type = institution:meritocracy
				value = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

# HOME按钮1右键：移除唯贤主义思潮
sakuya_loc_home_btn1_convert_culture_right = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_institution_progress = {
				type = institution:meritocracy
				value = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_shown = {
		always = yes

		always = yes

		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_research_boost_alpha_small_negative
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_research_boost_alpha_massive
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_research_boost_alpha_massive_negative
	}

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_military_boost_ultimate
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_military_boost_button
			value = 1
		}
	}
}

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_military_boost
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_military_boost_ultimate
		remove_variable = sakuya_military_boost_button
	}
}

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_parliament = yes
		is_active_parliament = no
	}
	effect = {
		start_parliament_effect = yes
	}
}

# 直接增加当前议会议题支持度
sakuya_parliament_issue_support_effect_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_active_parliament = yes
	}
	effect = {
		change_parliament_issue_support = {
			value = 0.1
		}
	}
}

# 直接减少当前议会议题支持度
sakuya_parliament_issue_support_effect_remove = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_active_parliament = yes
	}
	effect = {
		change_parliament_issue_support = {
			value = 0.1
			multiply = -1
		}
	}
}

# 当前议会议题支持度设为满值
sakuya_parliament_issue_support_effect_set_max = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_active_parliament = yes
	}
	effect = {
		set_parliament_issue_support = {
			value = 1
		}
	}
}

			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

# 按钮1 - 左键：目标国对玩家 +50
sakuya_refresh_character_mark_cache = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_character = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_character_mark_cache
				value = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_variable = sakuya_character_mark_cache
		}
	}
}

sakuya_check_character_marked = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_character_mark_cache
	}
	effect = {
	}
}

sakuya_character_set_adm_66 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = sakuya_adm_adjust
					value = 66
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = sakuya_adm_adjust
					add = {
						value = scope:this.adm
						multiply = -1
					}
				}
				add_adm = var:sakuya_adm_adjust
				remove_variable = sakuya_adm_adjust
			}
		}
	}
}

# 让标记角色加入标记国家统治者的宗族
sakuya_character_join_marked_country_dynasty = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = rch_marked_character
		rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
		save_scope_as = actor
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			ruler_or_regent ?= {
				save_scope_as = marked_country_ruler
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_country_ruler
			}
			every_country = {
				every_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					}
					change_dynasty = scope:marked_country_ruler.dynasty
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 让标记国家统治者加入标记角色的宗族
sakuya_marked_ruler_join_character_dynasty = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				save_scope_as = marked_character
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_character
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
				}
				ruler_or_regent ?= {
					change_dynasty = scope:marked_character.dynasty
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 角色生育/怀孕
sakuya_character_add_fertility = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = sakuya_character_fertility_plus
					years = -1
					mode = add_and_extend
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_fertility_plus
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_fertility_plus_large
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_fertility_minus
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_fertility_minus_large
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_force_pregnancy = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				is_female = yes
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			save_scope_as = sakuya_force_country
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					is_female = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = sakuya_target_character
				# 如果有配偶,配偶让标记角色怀孕
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = first_spouse
						first_spouse = {
							is_alive = yes
							NOT = {
								is_female = yes
							}
						}
					}
					first_spouse = {
						impregnate = scope:sakuya_target_character
					}
				}
				# 否则统治者让标记角色怀孕
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:sakuya_force_country = {
							exists = ruler
							ruler = {
								is_alive = yes
								NOT = {
									is_female = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
					scope:sakuya_force_country.ruler = {
						impregnate = scope:sakuya_target_character
					}
				}
				# 否则继承人让标记角色怀孕
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:sakuya_force_country = {
							exists = heir
							heir = {
								is_alive = yes
								NOT = {
									is_female = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
					scope:sakuya_force_country.heir = {
						impregnate = scope:sakuya_target_character
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 角色婚姻管理
sakuya_character_marry_ruler = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = sakuya_player_country
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = ruler
				ruler = {
					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
			scope:sakuya_player_country = {
				ruler = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_marry_target
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:sakuya_marry_target
			}
			every_country = {
				every_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
						NOT = {
							this = scope:sakuya_marry_target
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = sakuya_selected_spouse
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = first_spouse
						}
						divorce_character = first_spouse
					}
					scope:sakuya_marry_target = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = first_spouse
							}
							divorce_character = first_spouse
						}
						marry_character_ignore_blocks = scope:sakuya_selected_spouse
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_marry_heir = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = sakuya_player_country
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = heir
				heir = {
					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
			scope:sakuya_player_country = {
				heir = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_marry_target
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:sakuya_marry_target
			}
			every_country = {
				every_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
						NOT = {
							this = scope:sakuya_marry_target
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = sakuya_selected_spouse
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = first_spouse
						}
						divorce_character = first_spouse
					}
					scope:sakuya_marry_target = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = first_spouse
							}
							divorce_character = first_spouse
						}
						marry_character_ignore_blocks = scope:sakuya_selected_spouse
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_divorce_spouse = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					exists = first_spouse
				}
				divorce_character = first_spouse
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_block_marriage = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = sakuya_character_marriage_blocked
					years = -1
					mode = add_and_extend
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_block_lowborn_marriage = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = sakuya_character_lowborn_marriage_blocked
					years = -1
					mode = add_and_extend
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_clear_marriage_modifiers = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_marriage_blocked
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_lowborn_marriage_blocked
			}
		}
	}
}

# 设置统治者父母（根据性别）
sakuya_character_set_parent_ruler = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = ruler
				ruler = {
					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = sakuya_player_country
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = sakuya_player_ruler
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:sakuya_player_ruler
			}
			every_country = {
				every_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					}
					save_scope_as = sakuya_parent_target
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ruler_of = scope:sakuya_player_country
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_female = yes
						}
						scope:sakuya_player_ruler = {
							change_mother = scope:sakuya_parent_target
						}
					}
					else = {
						scope:sakuya_player_ruler = {
							change_father = scope:sakuya_parent_target
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 设置统治者配偶（右键）
sakuya_character_set_spouse = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = ruler
				ruler = {
					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = sakuya_player_country
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = sakuya_player_ruler
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:sakuya_player_ruler
			}
			every_country = {
				every_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					}
					save_scope_as = sakuya_spouse_target
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ruler_of = scope:sakuya_player_country
						}
					}
					else = {
						# 先清理双方现有配偶
						if = {
							limit = {
								exists = first_spouse
							}
							divorce_character = first_spouse
						}
						scope:sakuya_player_ruler = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									exists = first_spouse
								}
								divorce_character = first_spouse
							}
							marry_character = scope:sakuya_spouse_target
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 配偶父母
sakuya_character_set_parent_spouse = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = ruler
				ruler = {
					is_alive = yes
				}
				ruler = {
					exists = first_spouse
				}
				ruler = {
					first_spouse = {
						is_alive = yes
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = sakuya_player_country
			ruler = {
				first_spouse = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_player_spouse
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:sakuya_player_spouse
			}
			every_country = {
				every_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					}
					save_scope_as = sakuya_parent_target
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ruler_of = scope:sakuya_player_country
						}
					}
					else = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_female = yes
							}
							scope:sakuya_player_spouse = {
								change_mother = scope:sakuya_parent_target
							}
						}
						else = {
							scope:sakuya_player_spouse = {
								change_father = scope:sakuya_parent_target
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 玩家加入标记国的所有战争（同阵营，尝试接管）
sakuya_war_join_with_target = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_country
			}
			take_over_all_wars = scope:marked_country
			scope:marked_country = {
				every_current_war = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_war_scope
					ROOT = {
						join_war_with = {
							war = scope:sakuya_war_scope
							target = scope:marked_country
							call_in_subjects = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 玩家作为敌人加入标记国的所有战争
sakuya_war_join_against_target = {

	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_country
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				every_current_war = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_war_scope
					ROOT = {
						join_war_against = {
							war = scope:sakuya_war_scope
							target = scope:marked_country
							call_in_subjects = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					}

				set_bankruptcy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {

				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}

	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				save_scope_as = sakuya_heir_candidate
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:sakuya_heir_candidate
			}
			set_as_designated_heir = scope:sakuya_heir_candidate
		}
	}
}

					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				save_scope_as = sakuya_ruler_candidate
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_country
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:sakuya_ruler_candidate
					}
					set_new_foreign_ruler = scope:sakuya_ruler_candidate
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 更改角色文化为玩家国家文化
sakuya_character_change_culture_player = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				change_character_culture = scope:player_country.culture
			}
		}
	}
}

# 更改角色文化为标记国家文化
sakuya_character_change_culture_target = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {

				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:marked_country
					}
					change_character_culture = scope:marked_country.culture
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {

			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_health_recovery
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_health_recovery_large
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_health_wound
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_health_wound_large
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_forced_imprisonment
			}
		}
	}

sakuya_character_toggle_imprisonment = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character_modifier = sakuya_character_forced_imprisonment
					}
					remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_forced_imprisonment
				}
				else = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						modifier = sakuya_character_forced_imprisonment
						years = -1
						mode = add_and_extend
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_character_block_interactions = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = sakuya_character_salary_penalty_rightonclick
					years = -1
					mode = add_and_extend
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {

				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_trait = trait:just
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				has_trait = intricate_web_weaver
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					has_trait = intricate_web_weaver
				}
				remove_trait = trait:intricate_web_weaver
			}
		}
	}
}

		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
					has_trait = abrasive
				}
				remove_trait = trait:abrasive
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_boost_opinion_target_to_player = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮1 - 右键：玩家对目标国 +50
sakuya_boost_opinion_player_to_target = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:marked_country
			modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost
		}
	}
}

# 按钮1 - Shift+左键：目标国对玩家 +1000
sakuya_boost_opinion_target_to_player_large = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost_large
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮1 - Ctrl+左键：删除好感效果
sakuya_remove_boost_opinion = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = root
						modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost
					}
				}
				remove_opinion = {
					target = root
					modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = root
						modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost_large
					}
				}
				remove_opinion = {
					target = root
					modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost_large
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_opinion = {
					target = scope:marked_country
					modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost
				}
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = scope:marked_country
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_opinion = {
					target = scope:marked_country
					modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost_large
				}
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = scope:marked_country
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_boost_large
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮2 - 左键：目标国对玩家 -50
sakuya_reduce_opinion_target_to_player = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_penalty
			}
		}
	}
}

# 信任 - 目标国对玩家增加
sakuya_add_trust = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			add_trust_equilibrium = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_trust_boost
			}
		}
	}
}

# 信任 - 清除
sakuya_clear_trust = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trust = {
						target = root
						modifier = sakuya_trust_boost
					}
				}
				remove_trust_equilibrium = {
					target = root
					modifier = sakuya_trust_boost
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trust = {
						target = root
						modifier = sakuya_trust_penalty
					}
				}
				remove_trust_equilibrium = {
					target = root
					modifier = sakuya_trust_penalty
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_trust = {
					target = scope:marked_country
					modifier = sakuya_trust_boost
				}
			}
			remove_trust_equilibrium = {
				target = scope:marked_country
				modifier = sakuya_trust_boost
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_trust = {
					target = scope:marked_country
					modifier = sakuya_trust_penalty
				}
			}
			remove_trust_equilibrium = {
				target = scope:marked_country
				modifier = sakuya_trust_penalty
			}
		}
	}
}

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		create_relation = {
			first = root
			second = scope:marked_country
			type = relation_type:alliance
		}
	}
}

# 宣战 - 目标国对玩家宣战
sakuya_declare_war_target_to_player = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			random_list = {
				# 基础CB
				10 = {
					declare_war_with_cb = {
						target = root
						type = casus_belli:cb_none
					}
				}
				10 = {

						type = casus_belli:cb_war_from_event
					}
				}

					}
				}
				10 = {
					declare_war_with_cb = {
						target = root

					}
				}

				}

				}
				# 宗教战争

						type = casus_belli:cb_crusade
					}
				}
				6 = {
					declare_war_with_cb = {

		}
	}

# 刺杀 - 目标国家统治者
sakuya_assassinate_ruler = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:marked_country = {
					exists = ruler
				}
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				ruler = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_target_ruler
				}
			}
			kill_character = {
				target = scope:sakuya_target_ruler
				reason = assassination
				killer = root.ruler_or_regent
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = CHEAT_MENU_ASSASSINATE_NO_RULER
		}
	}
}

# 刺杀 - 目标国家继承人
sakuya_assassinate_heir = {
	scope = country

		always = yes
	}

			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:marked_country = {
					exists = heir
				}
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				heir = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_target_heir
				}
			}
			kill_character = {
				target = scope:sakuya_target_heir
				reason = assassination
				killer = root.ruler_or_regent
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = CHEAT_MENU_ASSASSINATE_NO_HEIR
		}
	}
}

# 刺杀 - 目标国家配偶
sakuya_assassinate_consort = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:marked_country = {
					has_consort = yes
				}
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				consort = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_target_consort
				}
			}
			kill_character = {
				target = scope:sakuya_target_consort
				reason = assassination
				killer = root.ruler_or_regent
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = CHEAT_MENU_ASSASSINATE_NO_CONSORT
		}
	}
}

# 刺杀 - 目标国家摄政
sakuya_assassinate_regent = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:marked_country = {
					has_regent = yes
				}
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				ruler_or_regent = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_target_regent
				}
			}
			kill_character = {
				target = scope:sakuya_target_regent
				reason = assassination
				killer = root.ruler_or_regent
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = CHEAT_MENU_ASSASSINATE_NO_REGENT
		}
	}
}

# 刺杀 - 一次性清理（摄政/配偶/统治者/继承人/内阁）
sakuya_assassinate_clean_house = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_regent = yes
				}
				ruler_or_regent = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_clean_regent
				}
				kill_character = {
					target = scope:sakuya_clean_regent
					reason = assassination
					killer = root.ruler_or_regent
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_consort = yes
				}
				consort = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_clean_consort
				}
				kill_character = {
					target = scope:sakuya_clean_consort
					reason = assassination
					killer = root.ruler_or_regent
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = ruler
				}
				ruler = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_clean_ruler
				}
				kill_character = {
					target = scope:sakuya_clean_ruler
					reason = assassination
					killer = root.ruler_or_regent
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_heir = yes
				}
				heir = {
					save_scope_as = sakuya_clean_heir
				}
				kill_character = {
					target = scope:sakuya_clean_heir
					reason = assassination
					killer = root.ruler_or_regent
				}
			}
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					owner = scope:marked_country
					in_cabinet = yes
				}
				kill_character = {
					target = this
					reason = assassination
					killer = root.ruler_or_regent
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 吞并 - 吞并目标国家
sakuya_annex_country = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			set_variable = no_annex_event_trigger
		}
		annex_country = {
			country = scope:marked_country
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 吞并玩家所有附属国（Shift+左键）
# ====================================
sakuya_annex_all_subjects = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_subject = {
			always = yes
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_subject = {
			set_variable = no_annex_event_trigger
			root = {
				annex_country = {
					country = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 标记被标记国家的统治者
sakuya_mark_ruler_of_marked_country = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			exists = ruler
			ruler = {
				is_alive = yes
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 先清除所有已标记的角色
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
		# 标记被标记国家的统治者
		scope:marked_country.ruler = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				value = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = rch_marked_character
			value = scope:recipient
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_character_mark_cache
			value = yes
		}
		rch_set_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
	}
}

# 标记被标记国家的继承人
sakuya_mark_heir_of_marked_country = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {

			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen

				is_alive = yes
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = rch_marked_character
		rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
		save_scope_as = actor

			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 先清除所有已标记的角色

			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
		# 标记被标记国家的继承人
		scope:marked_country.heir = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				value = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = rch_marked_character
			value = scope:recipient
		}

			name = sakuya_character_mark_cache
			value = yes
		}

	}
}

# 标记被标记国家统治者的配偶

		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			exists = ruler
			ruler = {
				is_alive = yes
				exists = first_spouse

					is_alive = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = rch_marked_character
		rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
		save_scope_as = actor
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 先清除所有已标记的角色
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
		# 标记统治者的配偶
		scope:marked_country.ruler.first_spouse = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				value = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = rch_marked_character
			value = scope:recipient
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_character_mark_cache
			value = yes
		}
		rch_set_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
	}
}

		}

		scope:marked_country.regent = {
			save_scope_as = recipient

				value = yes
			}

# 按钮10 - 停战（左键）：打破停战
sakuya_truce_break = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_truce_with = scope:marked_country
			}
			remove_truce_with = scope:marked_country
		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_truce_with = root
				}
				remove_truce_with = root
			}
		}
	}
}

# 将玩家国家设为标记国家的宿敌
sakuya_rival_add_player_for_target = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			add_rival = root
		}
	}
}

				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen

		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			change_culture = root.culture
		}
	}
}

# 将标记国家的文化改为文化选择器中标记的文化
sakuya_country_change_target_culture_selected = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_culture = {
			has_variable = sakuya_selected_culture
		}
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_culture = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_selected_culture
			}
			save_scope_as = sakuya_country_selected_culture
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			change_culture = scope:sakuya_country_selected_culture
		}
	}
}

# 控制力 - 增加
sakuya_control_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_control_modifier_large_positive
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

# 控制力 - 减少
sakuya_control_remove = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_control_modifier_large_negative
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

# 控制力 - 清空修正
sakuya_control_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_control_modifier_large_positive
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_control_modifier_large_negative
	}
}

# 角色能力修改 - 行政能力
sakuya_character_add_adm_1 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_adm = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_mil = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

# Alpha测试 - 永生功能
sakuya_alpha_add_immortal = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = sakuya_character_immortal
					years = -1
					mode = add_and_extend
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_alpha_remove_immortal = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_character_immortal
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_alpha_add_conqueror = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = sakuya_conqueror_blessing
					years = -1
					mode = add_and_extend
				}
				add_trait = trait:strategist
				add_mil = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_alpha_remove_conqueror = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				remove_character_modifier = sakuya_conqueror_blessing
				remove_trait = trait:strategist
				add_mil = -100
				add_mil = 65
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_alpha_kill_marked = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = choking_accident
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = fell_down_stairs
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = sudden_illness
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = food_poisoning
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = hunting_accident
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = heart_failure
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = fever
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = suicide
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = wild_animal
						}
					}
					10 = {
						kill_character = {
							target = this
							reason = vanished
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_variable = sakuya_character_mark_cache
	}
}

# ====================================
# 修正组SGUI - 人口与行政相关
# ====================================
# 1. 人口增长（local_population_growth）
sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_left = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_location_modifier = {
				mode = add_and_extend
				years = -1
				modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_right = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_location_modifier = {
				mode = add_and_extend
				years = -1
				modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_neg
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_ctrl_left = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth
			remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_small
			remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_large
			remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_neg
			remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_neg_large
		}
	}
}

sakuya_loc_mod_pop_growth_ctrl_right = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked

# ====================================
# 动态人口添加系统 - 人数+识字率组合 (6个数量级别 × 5个识字率 = 30个SGUI)
# ====================================
# 注释：由于代码量较大，这里只展示关键模式
# 实际包含：10/100/1K/10K/100K/1M 人口 × 0%/25%/50%/75%/100% 识字率
# ====================================
# 思潮系统 - 18个思潮的添加/移除功能
# ====================================
# 检查是否有标记地点（用于enabled控制）
sakuya_check_has_marked = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
	}
}

# 传统时代 (Age I)
# 1. 封建主义 (feudalism)
sakuya_inst_feudalism_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_institution_progress = {
				type = institution:feudalism
				value = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_inst_feudalism_remove = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_institution_progress = {
				type = institution:feudalism
				value = -100
			}
		}
	}
}

		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_institution_progress = {
				type = institution:global_trade
				value = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			change_raw_material = goods:wild_game
		}
	}
}

# ========================================
# 快速建造功能 - Quick Construction
# ========================================
# 添加100%建造速度加成
sakuya_builds_speed_100 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		# 先移除可能存在的最大速度加成
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_max
			}
			remove_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_max
		}
		# 添加100%建造速度
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_100
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

# 添加99999%建造速度加成
sakuya_builds_speed_max = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		# 先移除可能存在的100%速度加成
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_100
			}
			remove_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_100
		}
		# 添加最大建造速度
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_max
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

# 移除所有建造速度加成
sakuya_builds_speed_remove = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		OR = {
			has_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_100
			has_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_max
		}
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_100
			}
			remove_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_max
			}
			remove_country_modifier = sakuya_construction_speed_max
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# Holy Site Creation
# ====================================
sakuya_loc_create_holy_sites = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
				NOT = {
					has_variable = sakuya_holy_site_created
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_holy_site_created
				value = yes
			}
			create_holy_site = {
				name = sakuya_dynamic_holy_site
				type = episcopal_see
				importance = 5
				location = this
				religions = {
					scope:player_country.religion
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 删除玩家创建的圣地
sakuya_loc_destroy_player_holy_sites = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
			has_variable = sakuya_holy_site_created
		}
	}
	effect = {
		religion = {
			every_holy_site_in_religion = {
				limit = {
					location = {
						has_variable = sakuya_marked
						has_variable = sakuya_holy_site_created
					}
				}
				# 先移除变量，再删除圣地
				location = {
					remove_variable = sakuya_holy_site_created
				}
				destroy_holy_site = this
			}
		}
	}
}

# 删除所有圣地（包括游戏原生）
sakuya_loc_destroy_all_holy_sites = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		religion = {
			every_holy_site_in_religion = {
				limit = {
					location = {
						has_variable = sakuya_marked
					}
				}
				# 尝试在 holy_site scope 中调用 destroy_holy_site = this
				destroy_holy_site = this
				location = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = sakuya_holy_site_created
						}
						remove_variable = sakuya_holy_site_created
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 删除其他宗教的圣地（遍历所有宗教）
sakuya_loc_destroy_other_religion_holy_sites = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		# 遍历所有宗教
		every_religion = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					this = root.religion
				}
			}
			every_holy_site_in_religion = {
				limit = {
					location = {
						has_variable = sakuya_marked
					}
				}
				destroy_holy_site = this
			}
		}
	}
}

# RGO规模+1 (左键)
sakuya_rgo_size_add_1 = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_location_modifier = {
				mode = add_and_extend
				years = -1
				modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_1
			}
		}
	}
}

# RGO规模清空 (Ctrl+左键)
sakuya_rgo_size_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_1
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_10
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_10
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_neg_1
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_neg_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_neg_10
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_rgo_size_neg_10
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			add_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_999999
		}
	}
}

# 原材料产出清空 (Ctrl+左键)
sakuya_raw_output_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_marked
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_10
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_10
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_1000
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_1000
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_10
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_10
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_1000
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_1000
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_1
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_50
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_50
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_50
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_50
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_100
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_100
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_100
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_100
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_500
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_500
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_500
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_500
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_2000
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_2000
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_2000
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_2000
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_999999
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_999999
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_999999
				}
				remove_location_modifier = sakuya_loc_mod_raw_output_neg_999999
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

# ====================================
# 快捷按钮：全地点操作（无需标记）
# ====================================
# 同化：将玩家所有地点的人口文化转换为主流文化
sakuya_quick_assimilate_all = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			every_pop = {
				split_pop = {
					fraction = 1
					culture = scope:player_country.culture
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 皈依：将玩家所有地点的人口宗教转换为国教
sakuya_quick_convert_all = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		every_owned_location = {
			every_pop = {
				split_pop = {
					fraction = 1
					religion = scope:player_country.religion
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 整合：将玩家所有地点整合为核心
sakuya_quick_integrate_all = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		every_owned_location = {
			province = {
				change_province_integration = core
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

		is_human = yes
	}

				mode = add_and_extend
				years = -1
				modifier = sakuya_btn8_lazy_max_control_100
			}
			change_control = control_radical_bonus
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 阶层满意度修改 (Estate Satisfaction)
# 5种主要阶层: crown王权, nobles贵族, clergy教士, burghers市民, peasants平民
# ====================================
# 王权 (Crown Estate)
sakuya_estate_crown_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
		country_has_estate = estate_type:crown_estate
	}
	effect = {
		add_estate_satisfaction = {
			type = estate_type:crown_estate
			value = 0.2
		}
	}
}

sakuya_estate_crown_sub = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
		country_has_estate = estate_type:crown_estate
	}
	effect = {
		add_estate_satisfaction = {
			type = estate_type:crown_estate
			value = -0.2
		}
	}
}

# 所有阶层满意度满值

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		every_estate = {
			ROOT = {
				add_estate_satisfaction = {
					type = prev.estate_type
					value = 0.2
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 奇米 (Dhimmi Estate)

	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
		country_has_estate = estate_type:dhimmi_estate
	}
	effect = {
		add_estate_satisfaction = {
			type = estate_type:dhimmi_estate
			value = 0.2
		}
	}
}

# ========================================
# 全局阶层满意度 SGUI (add_estate_satisfaction 全部阶层)
# ========================================
sakuya_global_satisfaction_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		every_estate = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					estate_type = estate_type:crown_estate
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				add_estate_satisfaction = {
					type = prev.estate_type
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_global_satisfaction_sub = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		every_estate = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					estate_type = estate_type:crown_estate
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				add_estate_satisfaction = {
					type = prev.estate_type
					value = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# 全局满意度modifier
sakuya_global_satisfaction_mod_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_global_satisfaction_pos
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

sakuya_global_satisfaction_mod_sub = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_global_satisfaction_neg
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

sakuya_global_satisfaction_mod_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
	}
	effect = {
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_global_satisfaction_pos
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_global_satisfaction_neg
	}
}

# ========================================
# 阶层金钱 SGUI (add_gold_to_estate)
# ========================================
sakuya_estate_nobles_gold_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes		#原始字段 is_player = YES
		country_has_estate = estate_type:nobles_estate
	}
	effect = {
		add_gold_to_estate = {
			estate_type = estate_type:nobles_estate
			value = 100
		}
	}
}

	}

# 切换议会状态：议会废除
sakuya_parliament_abolished_toggle = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_abolished
			}
			remove_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_abolished
		}
		else = {
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = sakuya_parliament_abolished
				years = -1
				mode = add_and_extend
			}
		}
	}
}

# 议会状态修正：清空所有状态
sakuya_parliament_status_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_abolished
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_has_a_parliamentary_system
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_can_call_rural_parliaments
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_allow_assembly_parliament
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_allow_council_parliament
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_allow_autocratic_parliament
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_allow_estate_parliament
	}
}

# 增加议会持续时间 (+10%)
sakuya_parliament_duration_increase = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_parliament_duration_increase
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

		always = yes
	}

		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {

			modifier = sakuya_parliament_duration_reduce_shift
			years = -1

		}
	}

# 清空议会持续时间修正
sakuya_parliament_duration_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_duration_increase
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_duration_reduce
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_duration_increase_shift
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_parliament_duration_reduce_shift
	}
}

		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_cultural_tradition_modifier_remove_alt
		remove_country_modifier = sakuya_cultural_tradition_modifier_add_tiny
	}
}

# ========================================
# 宗教杂项 SGUI (Religion Miscellaneous)
# ========================================
sakuya_blocked_from_conversion_toggle = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_modifier = sakuya_blocked_from_conversion
			}
			remove_country_modifier = sakuya_blocked_from_conversion
		}
		else = {
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = sakuya_blocked_from_conversion
				years = -1
				mode = add_and_extend
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

# ====================================
# 天主教专属：给活着的统治者封圣（实验性）
# 正常游戏只能给死者封圣，这里尝试突破限制
# ====================================
sakuya_catholic_canonize_ruler = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:catholic
	}
	effect = {
		# 尝试给当前统治者封圣
		ruler = {
			make_saint = root
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 天主教专属：封圣标记的角色（右键）
# ====================================
sakuya_catholic_canonize_marked_character = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:catholic
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				make_saint = root
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 天主教专属：请求枢机（绕过条件）
# 直接触发教宗事件，无视等级、冷却、费用等限制
# ====================================
sakuya_catholic_request_cardinal = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:catholic
		country_exists = c:PAP
	}
	effect = {
		# 保存玩家为请求者
		save_scope_as = actor
		# 随机选择一个有大教堂但没有枢机席位的地点
		random_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				has_building = building_type:cathedral
				NOT = {
					has_building = building_type:seat_of_cardinal
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = target_location
		}
		# 如果没有符合条件的地点，则使用任意拥有的地点
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					exists = scope:target_location
				}
			}
			random_owned_location = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_building = building_type:seat_of_cardinal
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_location
			}
		}
		# 设置费用变量（教宗事件需要）
		set_variable = {
			name = cost_of_cardinal
			value = 33
		}
		# 直接向教宗发送请求事件
		c:PAP = {
			trigger_event_non_silently = catholic_flavor.1000
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 天主教专属：绝罚（对标记国家执行）
# 使用游戏原生excommunicate_effect效果
# ====================================
sakuya_catholic_excommunicate = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:catholic
		country_exists = c:PAP
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		# 获取标记的国家
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 对标记国家执行绝罚效果
		scope:marked_country = {
			excommunicate_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# 解除绝罚
sakuya_catholic_lift_excommunication = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:catholic
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		scope:marked_country = {
			lift_excommunication_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 伊斯兰专属：隐秘主义vs教法主义价值观
# 左=隐秘主义(-100), 右=教法主义(+100)
# ====================================
# 左键：隐秘主义拉满(-100)

			religion = religion:sunni
			religion = religion:shia
			religion = religion:ibadi
		}
	}
	effect = {
		set_societal_value = {
			type = mysticism_vs_jurisprudence
			value = -100
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

		}
	}

# ====================================

	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:miaphysite
	}
	effect = {
		ruler = {
			make_saint = root
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 合性派专属：封圣标记的角色（右键）
# ====================================
sakuya_miaphysite_canonize_marked_character = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		religion = religion:miaphysite
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				}
				make_saint = root
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 按钮4：社会价值观（Societal Values）
# 每个价值观：左键拉满左侧，右键拉满右侧
# ====================================
# 1. 中央集权 vs 地方分权
sakuya_sv_centralization_left = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		change_societal_value = {
			type = centralization_vs_decentralization
			value = -20
		}
	}
}

sakuya_sv_centralization_right = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		change_societal_value = {
			type = centralization_vs_decentralization
			value = 20
		}
	}
}

# 15. 汉化 vs 去汉化
sakuya_sv_sinicized_available = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		NOT = { culture ?= { has_culture_group = culture_group:chinese_group } }
		OR = {
			is_subject_of = c:CHI
			AND = {
				exists = capital
				capital = {
					OR = {
						sub_continent ?= sub_continent:east_asia
						sub_continent ?= sub_continent:south_east_asia
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				exists = international_organization:middle_kingdom
				is_member_of_international_organization = international_organization:middle_kingdom
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
	}
}

sakuya_sv_sinicized_left = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		NOT = { culture ?= { has_culture_group = culture_group:chinese_group } }
		OR = {
			is_subject_of = c:CHI
			AND = {
				exists = capital
				capital = {
					OR = {
						sub_continent ?= sub_continent:east_asia
						sub_continent ?= sub_continent:south_east_asia
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				exists = international_organization:middle_kingdom
				is_member_of_international_organization = international_organization:middle_kingdom
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		change_societal_value = {
			type = sinicized_vs_unsinicized
			value = -20
		}
	}
}

# 特殊：隐秘主义 vs 教法主义（伊斯兰专属，已存在）
# 使用已有的 sakuya_islamic_mysticism_max 和 sakuya_islamic_jurisprudence_max
# ====================================
# 隐藏功能：科技测试
# ====================================
sakuya_hidden_add_paper_printing = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_gold = 1000
		research_advance = advance_type:paper_workshop_advance
		research_advance = advance_type:printing_press_shop_advance
	}
}

# ===== 批量关系功能 =====
# HRE成员好感度+200（2年衰减）- 左键添加
sakuya_hre_opinion_boost = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		is_member_of_international_organization = international_organization:hre
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_member_of_international_organization = international_organization:hre
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_international_organization = international_organization:hre
				NOT = {
					this = root
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_mass_boost
			}
		}
	}
}

# HRE好感度移除 - 右键
sakuya_hre_opinion_remove = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_international_organization = international_organization:hre
				NOT = {
					this = root
				}
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_mass_boost
			}
		}
	}
}

# 同宗教国家好感度+200（2年衰减）- 左键添加
sakuya_same_religion_opinion_boost = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				religion = root.religion
				NOT = {
					this = root
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_mass_boost
			}
		}
	}
}
