# 移除上述好感 + 移除20个常见负面效果
sakuya_remove_all_negative_opinions = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					this = root
				}
			}
			# 移除作弊菜单添加的好感
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = sakuya_opinion_mass_boost
			}
			# 移除50+个常见负面效果（全部有衰减）
			# === 基础外交负面 ===
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_declared_war
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_broke_alliance
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_decline_alliance
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_dishonored_alliance
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_broke_tribute
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_declined_tribute_offer
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_insult
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_insulted
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_threatened_us
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_supported_rebels
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_caught_spying
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_sabotage_reputation
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_placed_privateers
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_bribed_mercenaries
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_default_loans
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_raided_our_borders
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_destroyed_building
			}
			# === 军事通行/贸易拒绝 ===
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_decline_military_access
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_decline_fleet_basing_rights
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_decline_guarantee
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_decline_influence_nation
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = opinion_broke_trade_access
			}
			remove_opinion = {
				target = root

# ===== 地缘政治：探索&发现 =====
# 解除北美洲迷雾 - 左键
sakuya_discover_north_america = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		is_human = yes
	}
	effect = {
		sub_continent:north_america = {
			every_location_in_sub_continent = {
				discover_location = root
			}
		}
	}
}

# 按钮1: 月度金币收入/支出
sakuya_btn7_gold_income_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = sakuya_monthly_gold_income
			years = -1
			mode = add_and_extend
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 国家重命名功能
# 说明：重命名功能通过自定义GUI实现
# 通过标记国家变量 (sakuya_get_TAG_choosen) 定位目标
# 然后打开 sakuya_change_name.gui 自定义窗口
# ====================================
# 备用方案：如果GUI datamodel方式不工作，使用这个scripted_gui
# 配合输入框使用控制台命令直接重命名
sakuya_rename_marked_country_alt = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		# 获取被标记的国家
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 缓存标记国作用域，供GUI读取
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_country
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = sakuya_rename_target_country
				value = scope:marked_country
			}
		}
	}
}

# 重命名玩家自己的国家（右键功能）
sakuya_rename_self_country = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		# 设置目标为玩家自己的国家
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_rename_target_country
			value = ROOT
		}
	}
}

# 执行重命名 - 使用自定义窗口和输入框
sakuya_execute_rename = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		# 获取被标记的国家
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 执行重命名
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:marked_country
			}
			scope:marked_country = {
				# 尝试方法1：使用 change_country_name 效果
				# 如果这个不工作，我们会需要使用控制台命令
				change_country_name = sakuya_new_country_name
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 文化好感度：目标国对我国文化好感+1（左键）
# ====================================
sakuya_cultural_opinion_target_to_player_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
			}
			save_scope_as = marked_country
		}
		# 标记国家主流文化对玩家主流文化好感+1
		scope:marked_country = {
			culture = {
				change_cultural_view = {
					target = scope:player_country.culture
					change = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 文化好感度：主流文化对接纳文化好感+1（Ctrl+左键）
# ====================================
sakuya_cultural_opinion_both_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_accepted_culture = {
			always = yes
		}
	}
	effect = {
		# 玩家主流文化对所有接纳文化好感+1
		every_accepted_culture = {
			change_cultural_view = {
				target = root.culture
				change = 1
			}
			root.culture = {
				change_cultural_view = {
					target = prev
					change = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================
# 文化好感度：主流文化对接纳文化好感-1（Ctrl+右键）

			}

	}
}

# ====================================
# 文化好感度：我国主流文化对所有完全相容文化（accepted）增加5点好感
# ====================================
sakuya_cultural_opinion_accepted_add = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		# 主流文化对所有相容文化好感+5
		culture = {
			scope:player_country = {
				every_accepted_culture = {
					prev = {
						change_cultural_view = {
							target = prev.culture
							change = 5
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# 所有相容文化对主流文化好感+5
		every_accepted_culture = {
			change_cultural_view = {
				target = scope:player_country.culture
				change = 5
			}
		}
	}
}

# ====================================

# ====================================

					}
				}

		}
		every_accepted_culture = {
			change_cultural_view = {

				change = -5
			}
		}
	}
}

# ============================================
# 清除全部标记变量 - Clear All Mark Variables
# ============================================
sakuya_clear_all_mark_variables = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = { always = yes }
	is_valid = { always = yes }
	effect = {
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked_country }
			remove_variable = sakuya_marked_country
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
			remove_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one }
				remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
		remove_variable = rch_marked_character
		remove_variable = sakuya_character_mark_cache
		rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
	}
}

# ====================================
# SAKUYA BETA - Character Marker System
# ====================================
sakuya_beta_mark_character = {
	scope = character
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
		}
	}
	effect = {
		owner = {
			remove_variable = rch_marked_character
			rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
		}
		# 清除所有国家内角色的旧标记
		every_country = {
			every_character = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one }
				remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
		owner = {
			every_character = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one }
				remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			}
		}
		# 标记当前角色
		save_scope_as = recipient
		set_variable = {
			name = sakuya_the_chosen_one
			value = yes
		}
		owner = {
			save_scope_as = actor
			set_variable = {
				name = rch_marked_character
				value = scope:recipient
			}
			rch_set_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
		}
	}
}

# 取消标记角色
sakuya_beta_unmark_character = {
	scope = character
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
		owner = {
			remove_variable = rch_marked_character
			rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
		}
	}
}

sakuya_prepare_dynasty_rename_window = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		any_country = {
			any_character = {
				has_variable = sakuya_the_chosen_one
				has_dynasty = yes
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		rch_prepare_dynasty_rename = yes
	}
}

sakuya_apply_dynasty_rename = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = rch_dynasty_to_be_renamed_selected
	}
	effect = {
		rch_rename_dynasty = yes
	}
}

sakuya_clear_dynasty_rename_window_state = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = rch_dynasty_to_be_renamed_selected
		rch_clear_dynasty_to_be_renamed = yes
	}
}

		}
	}
	effect = {
	}

sakuya_loc_popgen_type_clear = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_burghers
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_nobles
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_clergy
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_soldiers
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_peasants
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_tribesmen
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
		remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
	}
}

sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers_toggle = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers }
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_burghers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_nobles
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_clergy
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_soldiers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_peasants
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_tribesmen
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
		}
		else = {
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_burghers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_nobles
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_clergy
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_soldiers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_peasants
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_tribesmen
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
			set_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_type value = pop_type:laborers }
		}
	}
}

			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_nobles
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_clergy
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_soldiers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_peasants
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_tribesmen
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
		}
		else = {
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_laborers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_burghers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_nobles
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_clergy
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_soldiers
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_peasants
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_tribesmen
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
			remove_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
			set_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_type value = pop_type:slaves }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves_check = {

	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type_slaves
	}
	effect = {

	}
}

	scope = country

			has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen
		}
	}
	effect = {

	}
}

sakuya_loc_popgen_ready_check = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_amount_value
		has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
		OR = {
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_player
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_target
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_selector
		}
		OR = {
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_player
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_target
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_selector
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_target }
			any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_target }
			any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_selector }
			any_culture = { has_variable = sakuya_selected_culture }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_selector }
			any_religion = { has_variable = sakuya_selected_religion }
		}
	}
	effect = {

	}
}

sakuya_loc_popgen_spawn_marked = {
	scope = country
	is_shown = {
		always = yes
	}
	is_valid = {
		has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_amount_value
		has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_type
		OR = {
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_player
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_target
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_selector
		}
		OR = {
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_player
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_target
			has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_selector
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_target }
			any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_target }
			any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_selector }
			any_culture = { has_variable = sakuya_selected_culture }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_selector }
			any_religion = { has_variable = sakuya_selected_religion }
		}
		any_owned_location = {
			has_variable = sakuya_marked
		}
	}
	effect = {
		save_scope_as = player_country
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_player }
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture value = root.culture }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_target }
			random_country = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
				save_scope_as = marked_country
			}
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture value = scope:marked_country.culture }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture_selector }
			every_culture = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_selected_culture }
				save_scope_as = sakuya_loc_popgen_selected_culture
			}
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_culture value = scope:sakuya_loc_popgen_selected_culture }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_player }
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion value = root.religion }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_target }
			random_country = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_get_TAG_choosen }
				save_scope_as = marked_country
			}
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion value = scope:marked_country.religion }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion_selector }
			every_religion = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_selected_religion }
				save_scope_as = sakuya_loc_popgen_selected_religion
			}
			set_variable = { name = sakuya_loc_popgen_religion value = scope:sakuya_loc_popgen_selected_religion }
		}
		every_owned_location = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_marked }
			add_pop = {
				type = "scope:player_country.var:sakuya_loc_popgen_type"
				culture = "scope:player_country.var:sakuya_loc_popgen_culture"
				religion = "scope:player_country.var:sakuya_loc_popgen_religion"
				literacy = 0.5
				size = scope:player_country.var:sakuya_loc_popgen_amount_value
			}
		}
	}
}
